Implement the following plan:

# Remove transcript_path Storage and Compute Dynamically

## Context

Currently, we store `transcript_path` in session state for each agent session. The transcript path is provided by the agent (Claude or Gemini) in every hook call and stored in:
- `session.State.TranscriptPath` (in `.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json`)
- Used for mid-session commit detection to analyze live transcript files

**The Problem:**
Storing the transcript path is unnecessary because both Claude and Gemini use deterministic path schemes that can be computed from:
1. **Home directory** (`os.UserHomeDir()`)
2. **Repository path** (repo root)
3. **Session ID** (agent session UUID)
4. **Agent type** (to select the right path formula)

For Gemini, we'd also need the session start time, but that's already stored in `State.StartedAt`.

**Why this change:**
- Eliminates redundant storage
- Makes session data more portable (not tied to specific file system paths)
- Follows the pattern already used by `Agent.GetSessionDir(repoPath)` which computes paths deterministically
- Simplifies session initialization by removing one field to track

## Current State

### How transcript_path flows currently:

1. **Hook provides transcript_path** (from agent's JSON payload):
   - Claude: `transcript_path` in `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`, etc. hooks
   - Gemini: `transcript_path` in `BeforeAgent`, `AfterAgent`, etc. hooks

2. **Stored in session state** during `InitializeSession()`:
   - `/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go:1238-1239` - updates on session resume
   - `/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go:232` - sets on new session
   - `/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go:949` - sets in auto-commit strategy

3. **Used for mid-session commit detection**:
   - `/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go:967-1005` (`sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`)
   - Calls `agent.TranscriptAnalyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)`
   - Calls `agent.TranscriptAnalyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, ...)`

4. **Used during condensation**:
   - `/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go:126` - passed to `extractSessionData`
   - Condensation prefers live transcript over shadow branch copy for freshness

### Agent-specific path schemes:

**Claude Code** (`/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go:165-179`):
```
$HOME/.claude/projects/<sanitized-repo-path>/<session-id>.jsonl
```
- `sanitized-repo-path` = repo path with non-alphanumeric chars replaced by `-`
- Session ID is the agent's UUID (identity with Entire session ID)
- Extension: `.jsonl`

**Gemini CLI** (`/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go:173-189`):
```
$HOME/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized-repo-path>/chats/session-<date>-<id-prefix>.json
```
- `sanitized-repo-path` = repo path with non-alphanumeric chars replaced by `-` (same as Claude)
- Date format: `YYYY-MM-DDTHH-MM` (from session start time)
- ID prefix: first portion of session UUID
- Extension: `.json`

Note: Based on the comment in `/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go:12`, Gemini uses:
```
~/.gemini/tmp/<hash>/chats/session-<date>-<id>.json
```

The `<hash>` in the comment suggests a hash-based directory name, but the actual implementation in `SanitizePathForGemini()` uses the same dash-replacement as Claude, not a SHA256 hash like mentioned in the user's request.

## Proposed Changes

### 1. Add transcript path computation methods to Agent interface

**File:** `/cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go`

Add new method to `Agent` interface:
```go
// ComputeTranscriptPath returns the path where this agent stores session transcripts.
// Parameters:
//   - repoPath: absolute path to repository root
//   - sessionID: the agent's session UUID (NOT the legacy date-prefixed Entire ID)
//   - startTime: session start time (used for Gemini's date-based naming)
// Returns the absolute path to the transcript file.
ComputeTranscriptPath(repoPath, sessionID string, startTime time.Time) (string, error)
```

### 2. Implement ComputeTranscriptPath for each agent

**Claude Code** (`/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go`):
```go
// ComputeTranscriptPath returns the path to a Claude Code session transcript.
// Format: $HOME/.claude/projects/<sanitized-repo-path>/<session-id>.jsonl
func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ComputeTranscriptPath(repoPath, sessionID string, startTime time.Time) (string, error) {
	sessionDir, err := c.GetSessionDir(repoPath)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, sessionID+".jsonl"), nil
}
```

**Gemini CLI** (`/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go`):

User clarified:
- **Path format:** Use SHA256 hash of repo path (not dash replacement)
- **Session ID in filename:** First block until `-` is reached (first segment of UUID)

Example UUID: `a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890`
→ First block: `a1b2c3d4`

```go
// ComputeTranscriptPath returns the path to a Gemini CLI session transcript.
// Format: $HOME/.gemini/tmp/<sha256-hash>/chats/session-<date>-<id-prefix>.json
// Date format: YYYY-MM-DDTHH-MM (from startTime)
// ID prefix: First block of UUID (up to first dash)
func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ComputeTranscriptPath(repoPath, sessionID string, startTime time.Time) (string, error) {
	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
	}

	// SHA256 hash of repo path
	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(repoPath))
	projectHash := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])

	// Extract first block of session ID (up to first dash)
	idPrefix := sessionID
	if dashIdx := strings.IndexByte(sessionID, '-'); dashIdx > 0 {
		idPrefix = sessionID[:dashIdx]
	}

	// Format: session-2026-02-11T00-20-a1b2c3d4.json
	dateStr := startTime.Format("2006-01-02T15-04")
	filename := fmt.Sprintf("session-%s-%s.json", dateStr, idPrefix)

	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectHash, "chats", filename), nil
}
```

**Important:** This changes the current `GetSessionDir()` implementation which uses `SanitizePathForGemini()`. We need to update `GetSessionDir()` to also use SHA256 hashing to be consistent.

Remove:

Keep the JSON tag `transcript_path` deprecated in comments for backward compatibility documentation.

**Files to modify:**

### 5. Update code that reads transcript_path to compute it instead

**Mid-session commit detection** (`/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go:967-1005`):

Replace:
```go
if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
    return false, nil
}
// ...
currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
// ...
modifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, ...)
```

With:
```go
if state.AgentType == "" {
    return false, nil
}

// Get the agent for transcript analysis
ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
if err != nil {
    return false, nil
}

// Compute transcript path
repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
if err != nil {
    return false, nil
}
transcriptPath, err := ag.ComputeTranscriptPath(repoRoot, state.SessionID, state.StartedAt)
if err != nil {
    return false, nil
}

// Now use transcriptPath as before
currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(transcriptPath)
// ...
```

**Condensation** (`/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go:126`):

Similarly, compute the transcript path instead of using `state.TranscriptPath`:
```go
// Compute live transcript path for freshness
var liveTranscriptPath string
if state.AgentType != "" {
    if ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType); err == nil {
        if repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err == nil {
            liveTranscriptPath, _ = ag.ComputeTranscriptPath(repoRoot, state.SessionID, state.StartedAt)
        }
    }
}

sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
```

### 6. Update SaveContext and TaskCheckpointContext structs

**File:** `/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go`

In `SaveContext` (lines 145-188), the `TranscriptPath` field is set by hook handlers and passed to strategies. This flow should remain unchanged - the hook handlers receive the transcript path from the agent, but we just won't store it in session state.

The strategies will compute it when needed using the new `Agent.ComputeTranscriptPath()` method.

### 7. Test updates

**Files to check:**
- `/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/mid_session_commit_test.go:57-60` - Remove assertion on `TranscriptPath`
- Any other integration tests that check session state fields

### 8. Update Gemini's GetSessionDir() to use SHA256 hashing

**File:** `/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go:173-189`

Currently `GetSessionDir()` uses `SanitizePathForGemini()` which does dash replacement. Update it to use SHA256 hashing to match Gemini CLI's actual implementation:

```go
// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<sha256-hash>/chats/
func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
	// Check for test environment override
	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
		return override, nil
	}

	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
	}

	// SHA256 hash of repo path (matches Gemini CLI implementation)
	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(repoPath))
	projectHash := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])

	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectHash, "chats"), nil
}
```

This means we can deprecate `SanitizePathForGemini()` since it's no longer used.

## Verification Plan

After implementation:

1. **Unit tests:**
   - Add tests for `ComputeTranscriptPath()` in each agent implementation
   - Verify path format matches expected patterns
   - Test with various repo paths (with spaces, special chars, etc.)

2. **Integration tests:**
   - Run existing integration tests to ensure session functionality works
   - Specifically test `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript()` mid-session commit detection
   - Test session condensation with live transcript reading

3. **Manual testing:**
   - Initialize a Claude Code session
   - Make changes and verify mid-session commit detection works
   - Repeat with Gemini CLI
   - Verify session resume still works correctly

4. **Backward compatibility:**
   - Existing session state files with `transcript_path` will still load (field is optional)
   - New sessions won't write this field
   - Code gracefully handles both cases

## Summary of Changes

1. Add `ComputeTranscriptPath()` method to `Agent` interface
2. Implement for Claude Code (simple: `sessionDir/<id>.jsonl`)
3. Implement for Gemini CLI (complex: SHA256 hash + date format + ID prefix)
4. Update `GetSessionDir()` for Gemini to use SHA256 hashing (consistency fix)
5. Remove `TranscriptPath` field from `session.State`
6. Update all code that reads `state.TranscriptPath` to compute it instead
7. Update strategy initialization to not store transcript path
8. Update tests that check `TranscriptPath` field

**User answers incorporated:**
- ✅ Gemini ID prefix: First block until `-` (e.g., `a1b2c3d4` from `a1b2c3d4-e5f6-...`)
- ✅ Gemini path: SHA256 hash of repo path (not dash replacement)

    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→type Agent interface {
    14→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    15→	Name() AgentName
    16→
    17→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    18→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    19→	Type() AgentType
    20→

    22→	Description() string
    23→

    28→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    29→
    30→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks
    31→	SupportsHooks() bool
    32→

    35→

    38→
    39→	// TransformSessionID converts agent session ID to Entire session ID
    40→	TransformSessionID(agentSessionID string) string
    41→
    42→	// ExtractAgentSessionID extracts agent session ID from Entire ID
    43→	ExtractAgentSessionID(entireSessionID string) string
    44→
    45→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    46→	// Examples:
    47→	//   Claude: ~/.claude/projects/<sanitized-repo-path>/
    48→	//   Aider: current working directory (returns repoPath)
    49→	//   Cursor: ~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/globalStorage/
    50→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    51→
    52→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    53→	// Handles different formats: JSONL (Claude), SQLite (Cursor), Markdown (Aider)
    54→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    55→
    56→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.

    58→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error

    64→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    65→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    66→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
    67→type HookSupport interface {
    68→	Agent
    69→
    70→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
    71→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
    72→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    73→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    74→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
    75→
    76→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
    77→	UninstallHooks() error
    78→
    79→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
    80→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
    81→
    82→	// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports
    83→	GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
    84→}
    85→
    86→// HookHandler is implemented by agents that define their own hook vocabulary.
    87→// Each agent defines its own hook names (verbs) which become subcommands
    88→// under `entire hooks <agent>`. The actual handling is done by handlers
    89→// registered in the CLI package to avoid circular dependencies.
    90→//
    91→// This allows different agents to have completely different hook vocabularies
    92→// (e.g., Claude Code has "stop", Cursor might have "completion").
    93→type HookHandler interface {
    94→	Agent
    95→
    96→	// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    97→	// These are the subcommand names that will appear under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    98→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "pre-task", "post-task", "post-todo"]
    99→	GetHookNames() []string
   100→}
   101→
   102→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   103→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   104→// to detect session activity.
   105→type FileWatcher interface {
   106→	Agent
   107→
   108→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   109→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   110→
   111→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   112→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   113→}
   114→
   115→// TranscriptAnalyzer is implemented by agents that support transcript analysis.
   116→// This allows agent-agnostic detection of work done between checkpoints.
   117→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   118→	Agent
   119→
   120→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   121→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   122→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   123→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   124→	// Use this to efficiently check if the transcript has grown since last checkpoint.
   125→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   126→
   127→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   128→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   129→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   130→	// Returns:
   131→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   132→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   133→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   134→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   135→}
   136→
   137→// TranscriptChunker is implemented by agents that support transcript chunking.
   138→// This allows agents to split large transcripts into chunks for storage (GitHub has
   139→// a 100MB blob limit) and reassemble them when reading.
   140→type TranscriptChunker interface {
   141→	Agent
   142→
   143→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
   144→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
   145→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
   146→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
   147→
   148→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
   149→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
   150→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
   151→}

    83→// ParseHookInput parses Claude Code hook input from stdin.
    84→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   103→		var raw userPromptSubmitRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse user prompt submit: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   110→
   111→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   112→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   113→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   114→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   115→		}
   116→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   117→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookPreToolUse:
   120→		var raw taskHookInputRaw
   121→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   122→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse pre-tool input: %w", err)
   123→		}
   124→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   125→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   126→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   127→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   128→
   129→	case agent.HookPostToolUse:
   130→		var raw postToolHookInputRaw
   131→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse post-tool input: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   135→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   136→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   137→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   138→		// Store agent ID in raw data for Task tool results
   139→		if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   140→			input.RawData["agent_id"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   141→		}
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	return input, nil
   145→}
   146→
   147→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   148→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   149→	return input.SessionID
   150→}
   151→
   152→// TransformSessionID converts a Claude session ID to an Entire session ID.
   153→// This is now an identity function - the agent session ID IS the Entire session ID.
   154→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TransformSessionID(agentSessionID string) string {
   155→	return agentSessionID
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ExtractAgentSessionID extracts the Claude session ID from an Entire session ID.
   159→// Since Entire session ID = agent session ID (identity), this returns the input unchanged.
   160→// For backwards compatibility with legacy date-prefixed IDs, it strips the prefix if present.
   161→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractAgentSessionID(entireSessionID string) string {
   162→	return sessionid.ModelSessionID(entireSessionID)
   163→}
   164→
   165→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
   166→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   167→	// Check for test environment override
   168→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   169→		return override, nil
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   178→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   179→}
   180→
   181→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   182→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   183→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   184→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   185→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   186→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   190→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   196→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   202→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   203→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   204→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   205→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   206→		NativeData:    data,
   207→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   208→	}, nil
   209→}
   210→
   211→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   212→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   213→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   214→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   215→	if session == nil {
   216→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   220→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   221→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   225→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   229→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   233→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   234→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	return nil
   238→}
   239→
   240→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   241→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   242→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   243→}
   244→
   245→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   246→
   247→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   248→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   249→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   250→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   251→		return ""
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		return ""
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   260→}
   261→
   262→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   263→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   264→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   265→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   266→	if session == nil {
   267→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   271→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	if uuid == "" {
   275→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   276→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   277→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   278→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   279→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   280→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   281→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   282→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   283→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   284→		}, nil
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   288→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   289→	if err != nil {
   290→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   294→
   295→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   296→	if err != nil {
   297→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   301→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   302→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   303→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   304→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   305→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   306→		NativeData:    newData,
   307→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   308→	}, nil
   309→}
   310→
   311→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   312→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   313→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   314→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   315→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   316→		return "", false
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   320→	if err != nil {
   321→		return "", false
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   325→}
   326→
   327→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   328→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   329→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   330→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   331→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   332→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   333→	})
   334→}
   335→
   336→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   337→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   338→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   339→
   340→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   341→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   342→}
   343→
   344→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   345→
   346→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   347→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   348→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   349→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   350→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   351→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   352→	if path == "" {
   353→		return 0, nil
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   357→	if err != nil {
   358→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   359→			return 0, nil
   360→		}
   361→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   362→	}
   363→	defer file.Close()
   364→
   365→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   366→	lineCount := 0
   367→
   368→	for {
   369→		_, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   370→		if err != nil {
   371→			if err == io.EOF {
   372→				break
   373→			}
   374→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   375→		}
   376→		lineCount++
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	return lineCount, nil
   380→}
   381→
   382→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   383→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   384→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   385→// Returns:
   386→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   387→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   388→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   389→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   390→	if path == "" {
   391→		return nil, 0, nil
   392→	}
   393→
   394→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   395→	if openErr != nil {
   396→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   397→	}
   398→	defer file.Close()
   399→
   400→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   401→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   402→	lineNum := 0
   403→
   404→	for {
   405→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   406→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   407→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   408→		}
   409→
   410→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   411→			lineNum++
   412→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   413→				var line TranscriptLine
   414→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   415→					lines = append(lines, line)
   416→				}
   417→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   418→			}
   419→		}
   420→
   421→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   422→			break
   423→		}
   424→	}
   425→
   426→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   427→}
   428→
   429→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   430→
   431→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   432→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   433→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   434→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   435→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→	return chunks, nil
   440→}
   441→
   442→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   443→//
   444→//nolint:unparam // error return is required by interface, kept for consistency
   445→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   446→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   447→}

    27→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.

    30→type GeminiCLIAgent struct{}

    51→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    52→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    53→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    54→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    55→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    56→	if err != nil {
    57→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    58→		repoRoot = "."
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Check for .gemini directory
    62→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    63→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    64→		return true, nil
    65→	}
    66→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    67→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    68→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    69→		return true, nil
    70→	}
    71→	return false, nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Gemini's hook config file.
    75→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    76→	return ".gemini/settings.json"
    77→}

    79→// SupportsHooks returns true as Gemini CLI supports lifecycle hooks.
    80→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    81→	return true
    82→}
    83→
    84→// ParseHookInput parses Gemini CLI hook input from stdin.
    85→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    86→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    87→	if err != nil {
    88→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	if len(data) == 0 {
    92→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    96→		HookType:  hookType,
    97→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    98→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	// Parse based on hook type
   102→	switch hookType {
   103→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   104→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   105→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   106→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   107→		}
   108→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   109→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   110→		// Store Gemini-specific fields in RawData
   111→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   112→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   113→		if raw.Source != "" {
   114→			input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   115→		}
   116→		if raw.Reason != "" {
   117→			input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   118→		}
   119→
   120→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   121→		// BeforeAgent is Gemini's equivalent to Claude's UserPromptSubmit
   122→		// It provides the user's prompt in the "prompt" field
   123→		var raw agentHookInputRaw
   124→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   125→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse agent hook input: %w", err)
   126→		}
   127→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   128→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   129→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   130→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   131→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   132→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   133→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   134→		}
   135→
   136→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   137→		var raw toolHookInputRaw
   138→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   139→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   140→		}
   141→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   142→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   143→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   144→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   145→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   146→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResponse
   147→		}
   148→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   149→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	return input, nil
   153→}

   173→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   174→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   175→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   176→	// Check for test environment override
   177→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   178→		return override, nil
   179→	}

   181→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()

   191→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   192→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   193→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   194→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   195→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Read the raw JSON file
   199→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   205→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   208→		modifiedFiles = nil
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   212→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   213→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   214→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   215→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   216→		NativeData:    data,
   217→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   218→	}, nil
   219→}
   220→
   221→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   222→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   223→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   224→	if session == nil {
   225→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   229→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   234→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   238→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Write the raw JSON data
   242→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   243→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	return nil
   247→}
   248→
   249→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   250→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   251→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   252→}
   253→
   254→// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
   255→// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
   256→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   257→
   258→func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
   259→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   260→}
   261→
   262→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   263→
   264→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   265→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   266→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   267→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   268→	if path == "" {
   269→		return 0, nil
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   273→	if err != nil {
   274→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   275→			return 0, nil
   276→		}
   277→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	if len(data) == 0 {
   281→		return 0, nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   290→}
   291→
   292→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   293→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   294→// Returns:
   295→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   296→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   297→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   298→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   299→	if path == "" {
   300→		return nil, 0, nil
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   304→	if readErr != nil {
   305→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   306→			return nil, 0, nil
   307→		}
   308→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	if len(data) == 0 {
   312→		return nil, 0, nil
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   316→	if parseErr != nil {
   317→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   321→
   322→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   323→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   324→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   325→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   326→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   327→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   328→			continue
   329→		}
   330→
   331→		// Process tool calls in this message
   332→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   333→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   334→			isModifyTool := false
   335→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   336→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   337→					isModifyTool = true
   338→					break
   339→				}
   340→			}
   341→
   342→			if !isModifyTool {
   343→				continue
   344→			}
   345→
   346→			// Extract file path from args map
   347→			var file string
   348→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   349→				file = fp
   350→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   351→				file = p
   352→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   353→				file = fn
   354→			}
   355→
   356→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   357→				fileSet[file] = true
   358→				files = append(files, file)
   359→			}
   360→		}
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   364→}
   365→
   366→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   367→
   368→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   369→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   370→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   371→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   372→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   373→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   374→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   375→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   376→		if chunkErr != nil {
   377→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   378→		}
   379→		return chunks, nil
   380→	}
   381→
   382→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   383→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	var chunks [][]byte
   387→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   388→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   389→
   390→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   391→		// Marshal message to get its size
   392→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   393→		if err != nil {
   394→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   395→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   396→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   397→			)
   398→			continue
   399→		}
   400→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   401→
   402→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   403→			// Save current chunk
   404→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   405→			if err != nil {
   406→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   407→			}
   408→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   409→
   410→			// Start new chunk
   411→			currentMessages = nil
   412→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   413→		}
   414→
   415→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   416→		currentSize += msgSize
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	// Add the last chunk
   420→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   421→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   422→		if err != nil {
   423→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   424→		}
   425→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   429→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   430→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	return chunks, nil
   434→}
   435→
   436→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   437→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   438→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   439→
   440→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   441→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   442→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   443→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   444→		}
   445→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   446→	}
   447→
   448→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   449→	if err != nil {
   450→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   451→	}
   452→	return result, nil
   453→}
   454→

    24→// State represents the state of an active session.
    25→// This is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
    26→type State struct {
    27→	// SessionID is the unique session identifier
    28→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    29→
    30→	// CLIVersion is the version of the CLI that created this session
    31→	CLIVersion string `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
    32→
    33→	// BaseCommit tracks the current shadow branch base. Initially set to HEAD when the
    34→	// session starts, but updated on migration (pull/rebase) and after condensation.
    35→	// Used for shadow branch naming and checkpoint storage — NOT for attribution.
    36→	BaseCommit string `json:"base_commit"`
    37→
    38→	// AttributionBaseCommit is the commit used as the reference point for attribution calculations.
    39→	// Unlike BaseCommit (which tracks the shadow branch and moves with migration), this field
    40→	// preserves the original base commit so deferred condensation can correctly calculate
    41→	// agent vs human line attribution. Updated only after successful condensation.
    42→	AttributionBaseCommit string `json:"attribution_base_commit,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// WorktreePath is the absolute path to the worktree root
    45→	WorktreePath string `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"`
    46→
    47→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
    48→	// Derived from .git/worktrees/<name>/, stable across git worktree move
    49→	WorktreeID string `json:"worktree_id,omitempty"`
    50→
    51→	// StartedAt is when the session was started
    52→	StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at"`
    53→
    54→	// EndedAt is when the session was explicitly closed by the user.
    55→	// nil means the session is still active or was not cleanly closed.
    56→	EndedAt *time.Time `json:"ended_at,omitempty"`
    57→
    58→	// Phase is the lifecycle stage of this session (see phase.go).
    59→	// Empty means idle (backward compat with pre-state-machine files).
    60→	Phase Phase `json:"phase,omitempty"`
    61→
    62→	// PendingCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID for the current commit cycle.
    63→	// Generated once when first needed, reused across all commits in the session.
    64→	PendingCheckpointID string `json:"pending_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    65→
    66→	// LastInteractionTime is updated on every hook invocation.
    67→	// Used for stale session detection in "entire doctor".
    68→	LastInteractionTime *time.Time `json:"last_interaction_time,omitempty"`
    69→
    70→	// StepCount is the number of checkpoints/steps created in this session.
    71→	// JSON tag kept as "checkpoint_count" for backward compatibility with existing state files.
    72→	StepCount int `json:"checkpoint_count"`
    73→
    74→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart is the transcript line offset where the current
    75→	// checkpoint cycle began. Set to 0 at session start, updated to current
    76→	// transcript length after each condensation. Used to scope the transcript
    77→	// for checkpoint condensation: "everything since last checkpoint".
    78→	CheckpointTranscriptStart int `json:"checkpoint_transcript_start,omitempty"`
    79→

    81→	// Kept for backward compatibility with existing state files.

    83→	CondensedTranscriptLines int `json:"condensed_transcript_lines,omitempty"`
    84→
    85→	// UntrackedFilesAtStart tracks files that existed at session start (to preserve during rewind)
    86→	UntrackedFilesAtStart []string `json:"untracked_files_at_start,omitempty"`
    87→
    88→	// FilesTouched tracks files modified/created/deleted during this session

    91→	// LastCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID from last condensation, reused for subsequent commits without new content

    93→
    94→	// AgentType identifies the agent that created this session (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI", "Cursor")
    95→	AgentType agent.AgentType `json:"agent_type,omitempty"`
    96→
    97→	// Token usage tracking (accumulated across all checkpoints in this session)

    99→
   100→	// Deprecated: TranscriptLinesAtStart is replaced by CheckpointTranscriptStart.

   103→
   104→	// TranscriptIdentifierAtStart is the last transcript identifier when the session started.
   105→	// Used for identifier-based transcript scoping (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini).
   106→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string `json:"transcript_identifier_at_start,omitempty"`

   110→

   114→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.
   115→	// This enables accurate attribution by capturing user edits between checkpoints.

   117→

   122→
   123→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt.
   124→// By recording what changed since the last checkpoint BEFORE the agent works,
   125→// we can accurately separate user edits from agent contributions.
   126→type PromptAttribution struct {
   127→	// CheckpointNumber is which checkpoint this was recorded before (1-indexed)
   128→	CheckpointNumber int `json:"checkpoint_number"`
   129→
   130→	// UserLinesAdded is lines added by user since the last checkpoint
   131→	UserLinesAdded int `json:"user_lines_added"`
   132→
   133→	// UserLinesRemoved is lines removed by user since the last checkpoint
   134→	UserLinesRemoved int `json:"user_lines_removed"`
   135→
   136→	// AgentLinesAdded is total agent lines added so far (base → last checkpoint).
   137→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   138→	AgentLinesAdded int `json:"agent_lines_added"`
   139→
   140→	// AgentLinesRemoved is total agent lines removed so far (base → last checkpoint).
   141→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   142→	AgentLinesRemoved int `json:"agent_lines_removed"`
   143→
   144→	// UserAddedPerFile tracks per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking.
   145→	// This enables distinguishing user self-modifications from agent modifications.
   146→	// See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
   147→	UserAddedPerFile map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"`
   148→}
   149→
   150→// NormalizeAfterLoad applies backward-compatible migrations to state loaded from disk.
   151→// Call this after deserializing a State from JSON.
   152→func (s *State) NormalizeAfterLoad() {
   153→	// Migrate transcript fields: CheckpointTranscriptStart replaces both
   154→	// CondensedTranscriptLines and TranscriptLinesAtStart from older state files.
   155→	if s.CheckpointTranscriptStart == 0 {
   156→		if s.CondensedTranscriptLines > 0 {
   157→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.CondensedTranscriptLines
   158→		} else if s.TranscriptLinesAtStart > 0 {
   159→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   160→		}
   161→	}
   162→	// Clear deprecated fields so they aren't re-persisted.
   163→	// Note: this is a one-way migration. If the state is re-saved, older CLI versions
   164→	// will see 0 for these fields and fall back to scoping from the transcript start.
   165→	// This is acceptable since CLI upgrades are monotonic and the worst case is
   166→	// redundant transcript content in a condensation, not data loss.
   167→	s.CondensedTranscriptLines = 0
   168→	s.TranscriptLinesAtStart = 0
   169→
   170→	// Backfill AttributionBaseCommit for sessions created before this field existed.
   171→	// Without this, a mid-turn commit would migrate BaseCommit and the fallback in
   172→	// calculateSessionAttributions would use the migrated value, producing zero attribution.
   173→	if s.AttributionBaseCommit == "" && s.BaseCommit != "" {
   174→		s.AttributionBaseCommit = s.BaseCommit
   175→	}
   176→}
   177→
   178→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
   179→//
   180→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
   181→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
   182→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
   183→//
   184→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
   185→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
   186→type StateStore struct {
   187→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
   188→	stateDir string
   189→}
   190→
   191→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
   192→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
   193→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
   194→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→	return &StateStore{
   199→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, SessionStateDirName),
   200→	}, nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
   204→// This is useful for testing.
   205→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
   206→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   207→}
   208→
   209→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   210→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   211→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   212→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   213→
   214→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   215→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   216→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   220→
   221→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   222→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   223→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   224→	}
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	var state State
   230→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   231→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→	state.NormalizeAfterLoad()
   234→	return &state, nil
   235→}
   236→
   237→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   238→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   239→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   240→
   241→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   242→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   243→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   256→
   257→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   258→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   259→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   260→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   261→	}
   262→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   263→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   264→	}
   265→	return nil
   266→}
   267→
   268→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   269→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   270→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   271→
   272→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   273→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   278→
   279→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   280→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   281→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   282→		}
   283→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   284→	}
   285→	return nil
   286→}
   287→
   288→// RemoveAll removes the entire session state directory.
   289→// This is used during uninstall to completely remove all session state.
   290→func (s *StateStore) RemoveAll() error {
   291→	if err := os.RemoveAll(s.stateDir); err != nil {
   292→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state directory: %w", err)
   293→	}
   294→	return nil
   295→}
   296→
   297→// List returns all session states.
   298→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   299→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   300→
   301→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   302→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   303→		return nil, nil
   304→	}
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	var states []*State
   310→	for _, entry := range entries {
   311→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   312→			continue
   313→		}
   314→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   315→			continue // Skip temp files
   316→		}
   317→
   318→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   319→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   320→		if err != nil {
   321→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   322→		}
   323→		if state == nil {
   324→			continue
   325→		}
   326→
   327→		states = append(states, state)
   328→	}
   329→	return states, nil
   330→}
   331→
   332→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   333→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   334→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   335→}
   336→
   337→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   338→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   339→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   340→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   341→	ctx := context.Background()
   342→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   343→	cmd.Dir = "."
   344→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   345→	if err != nil {
   346→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   350→
   351→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   352→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   353→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   354→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   358→}
   359→

   303→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   304→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   305→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   306→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   307→// This handles the case where SaveChanges was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   308→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   309→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   310→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   311→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)

   325→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   326→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   327→	// (SaveChanges is only called when there are file modifications).
   328→	var fullTranscript string
   329→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   330→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   331→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   332→		}
   333→	}
   334→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   335→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   336→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   337→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   338→				fullTranscript = content
   339→			}

     1→// Package strategy provides an interface for different git strategies
     2→// that can be used to save and manage Claude Code session changes.
     3→package strategy
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"strings"
    10→	"time"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// ErrNoMetadata is returned when a commit does not have an Entire metadata trailer.
    18→var ErrNoMetadata = errors.New("commit has no entire metadata")
    19→
    20→// ErrNoSession is returned when no session info is available.
    21→var ErrNoSession = errors.New("no session info available")
    22→
    23→// ErrNotTaskCheckpoint is returned when a rewind point is not a task checkpoint.
    24→var ErrNotTaskCheckpoint = errors.New("not a task checkpoint")
    25→
    26→// ErrNotImplemented is returned when a feature is not yet implemented.
    27→var ErrNotImplemented = errors.New("not implemented")
    28→
    29→// SessionIDConflictError is returned when trying to start a new session
    30→// but the shadow branch already has commits from a different session ID.
    31→// This prevents orphaning existing session work.
    32→type SessionIDConflictError struct {
    33→	ExistingSession string // Session ID found in the shadow branch
    34→	NewSession      string // Session ID being initialized
    35→	ShadowBranch    string // The shadow branch name (e.g., "entire/abc1234")
    36→}
    37→
    38→func (e *SessionIDConflictError) Error() string {
    39→	return "session ID conflict: shadow branch has commits from a different session"
    40→}
    41→
    42→// ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir extracts the ToolUseID from a task metadata directory path.
    43→// Task metadata dirs have format: .entire/metadata/<session>/tasks/<toolUseID>
    44→// Returns empty string if not a task metadata directory.
    45→func ExtractToolUseIDFromTaskMetadataDir(metadataDir string) string {
    46→	parts := strings.Split(metadataDir, "/")
    47→	if len(parts) >= 2 && parts[len(parts)-2] == "tasks" {
    48→		return parts[len(parts)-1]
    49→	}
    50→	return ""
    51→}
    52→
    53→// SessionInfo contains information about the current session state.
    54→// This is used to generate trailers for linking commits to their AI session.
    55→type SessionInfo struct {
    56→	// SessionID is the session identifier extracted from the latest commit's metadata
    57→	SessionID string
    58→
    59→	// Reference is a strategy-specific reference string.
    60→	// For manual-commit strategy: "entire/abc1234" (the shadow branch name)
    61→	// Empty for commit strategy (metadata is in the same commit).
    62→	Reference string
    63→
    64→	// CommitHash is the full SHA of the commit containing the session metadata.
    65→	// Empty for commit strategy.
    66→	CommitHash string
    67→}
    68→
    69→// RewindPoint represents a point to which the user can rewind.
    70→// This abstraction allows different strategies to use different
    71→// identifiers (commit hashes, branch names, stash refs, etc.)
    72→type RewindPoint struct {
    73→	// ID is the unique identifier for this rewind point
    74→	// (commit hash, branch name, stash ref, etc.)
    75→	ID string
    76→
    77→	// Message is the human-readable description/summary
    78→	Message string
    79→
    80→	// MetadataDir is the path to the metadata directory
    81→	MetadataDir string
    82→
    83→	// Date is when this rewind point was created
    84→	Date time.Time
    85→
    86→	// IsTaskCheckpoint indicates if this is a task checkpoint (vs a session checkpoint)
    87→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool
    88→
    89→	// ToolUseID is the tool use ID for task checkpoints (empty for session checkpoints)
    90→	ToolUseID string
    91→
    92→	// IsLogsOnly indicates this is a commit with session logs but no shadow branch state.
    93→	// The logs can be restored from entire/checkpoints/v1, but file state requires git checkout.
    94→	IsLogsOnly bool
    95→
    96→	// CheckpointID is the stable 12-hex-char identifier for logs-only points.
    97→	// Used to retrieve logs from entire/checkpoints/v1/<id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/full.jsonl
    98→	// Empty for shadow branch checkpoints (uncommitted).
    99→	CheckpointID id.CheckpointID
   100→
   101→	// Agent is the human-readable name of the agent that created this checkpoint
   102→	// (e.g., "Claude Code", "Cursor")
   103→	Agent agent.AgentType
   104→
   105→	// SessionID is the session identifier for this checkpoint.
   106→	// Used to distinguish checkpoints from different concurrent sessions.
   107→	SessionID string
   108→
   109→	// SessionPrompt is the initial prompt that started this session.
   110→	// Used to help users identify which session a checkpoint belongs to.
   111→	SessionPrompt string
   112→
   113→	// SessionCount is the number of sessions in this checkpoint (1 for single-session).
   114→	// Only populated for logs-only points with multi-session checkpoints.
   115→	SessionCount int
   116→
   117→	// SessionIDs contains all session IDs when this is a multi-session checkpoint.
   118→	// The last entry is the most recent session (same as SessionID).
   119→	// Only populated for logs-only points with multi-session checkpoints.
   120→	SessionIDs []string

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go",
  "old_string": "// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.\n// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/\nfunc (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {\n\t// Check for test environment override\n\tif override := os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR\"); override != \"\" {\n\t\treturn override, nil\n\t}\n\n\thomeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get home directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name\n\tprojectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)\n\treturn filepath.Join(homeDir, \".gemini\", \"tmp\", projectDir, \"chats\"), nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.\n// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<sha256-hash>/chats/\nfunc (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {\n\t// Check for test environment override\n\tif override := os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR\"); override != \"\" {\n\t\treturn override, nil\n\t}\n\n\thomeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get home directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// SHA256 hash of repo path (matches Gemini CLI implementation)\n\thash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(repoPath))\n\tprojectHash := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])\n\n\treturn filepath.Join(homeDir, \".gemini\", \"tmp\", projectHash, \"chats\"), nil\n}\n\n// ComputeTranscriptPath returns the path to a Gemini CLI session transcript.\n// Format: $HOME/.gemini/tmp/<sha256-hash>/chats/session-<date>-<id-prefix>.json\n// Date format: YYYY-MM-DDTHH-MM (from startTime)\n// ID prefix: First block of UUID (up to first dash)\nfunc (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ComputeTranscriptPath(repoPath, sessionID string, startTime time.Time) (string, error) {\n\tsessionDir, err := g.GetSessionDir(repoPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract first block of session ID (up to first dash)\n\tidPrefix := sessionID\n\tif dashIdx := strings.IndexByte(sessionID, '-'); dashIdx > 0 {\n\t\tidPrefix = sessionID[:dashIdx]\n\t}\n\n\t// Format: session-2026-02-11T00-20-a1b2c3d4.json\n\tdateStr := startTime.Format(\"2006-01-02T15-04\")\n\tfilename := fmt.Sprintf(\"session-%s-%s.json\", dateStr, idPrefix)\n\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDir, filename), nil\n}"
}
```

     4→	"os"

     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→	_ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode" // Register Claude Code agent for transcript analysis
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
    15→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    16→	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    17→	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
    18→)
    19→
    20→// TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_NormalizesAbsolutePaths verifies
    21→// that sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript correctly normalizes absolute paths
    22→// from the transcript to repo-relative paths before comparing with staged files.
    23→//
    24→// Bug: ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset returns absolute paths (e.g., /tmp/repo/src/main.go)
    25→// but getStagedFiles returns repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go). The exact-string
    26→// comparison in hasOverlappingFiles never matches, causing "no content to link".
    27→func TestSessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript_NormalizesAbsolutePaths(t *testing.T) {
    28→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    29→	t.Chdir(dir)
    30→
    31→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    32→	require.NoError(t, err)
    33→
    34→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    35→
    36→	// Create a file that we'll stage
    37→	srcDir := filepath.Join(dir, "src")
    38→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(srcDir, 0o755))
    39→	testFile := filepath.Join(srcDir, "main.go")
    40→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main\n"), 0o644))
    41→
    42→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
    43→	require.NoError(t, err)
    44→	_, err = wt.Add("src/main.go")
    45→	require.NoError(t, err)
    46→
    47→	// Get the resolved worktree path first — on macOS, t.TempDir() returns /var/...
    48→	// but git resolves symlinks to /private/var/... . Claude Code uses the resolved
    49→	// path in its transcript, so we must too.
    50→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
    51→	require.NoError(t, err)
    52→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
    53→	require.NoError(t, err)
    54→
    55→	// Create a transcript file that references the file by absolute path
    56→	// (this is what Claude Code does — tool_use Write has absolute file_path)
    57→	absFilePath := filepath.Join(worktreePath, "src", "main.go")
    58→	transcriptContent := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"write a main.go file"}}
    59→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"` + absFilePath + `","content":"package main\n"}}]}}
    60→`
    61→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(dir, "transcript.jsonl")
    62→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(transcriptContent), 0o644))
    63→
    64→	// Create session state: no shadow branch (it was deleted after last condensation),
    65→	// transcript path points to the file, agent type is Claude Code
    66→	now := time.Now()
    67→
    68→	head, err := repo.Head()
    69→	require.NoError(t, err)
    70→
    71→	state := &SessionState{
    72→		SessionID:                 "test-abs-path-normalize",
    73→		BaseCommit:                head.Hash().String(),
    74→		WorktreePath:              worktreePath,
    75→		WorktreeID:                worktreeID,
    76→		StartedAt:                 now,
    77→		Phase:                     session.PhaseActive,
    78→		LastInteractionTime:       &now,
    79→		AgentType:                 agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode,
    80→		CheckpointTranscriptStart: 0, // No prior condensation
    81→	}
    82→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    83→
    84→	// Call sessionHasNewContent — should fall through to live transcript check
    85→	// since there's no shadow branch
    86→	hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
    87→	require.NoError(t, err)
    88→	assert.True(t, hasNew,
    89→		"sessionHasNewContent should return true when transcript has absolute paths "+
    90→			"that match repo-relative staged files after normalization")
    91→}
    92→
    93→// TestPostCommit_NoTrailer_UpdatesBaseCommit verifies that when a commit has no
    94→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer, PostCommit still updates BaseCommit for active sessions.
    95→//
    96→// Bug: PostCommit early-returns when no trailer is found (line ~530-536). EventGitCommit
    97→// never fires, BaseCommit never updates. All subsequent commits fail the
    98→// BaseCommit == currentHeadHash filter in PrepareCommitMsg.
    99→func TestPostCommit_NoTrailer_UpdatesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
   100→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)

   616→// CreateGeminiTranscript creates a Gemini JSON transcript file for the session.
   617→func (s *GeminiSession) CreateGeminiTranscript(prompt string, changes []FileChange) string {
   618→	// Build Gemini-format transcript (JSON, not JSONL)
   619→	messages := []map[string]interface{}{

   621→			"type":    "user",
   622→			"content": prompt,
   623→		},

   633→			"name": "write_file",
   634→			"input": map[string]string{
   635→				"path":    change.Path,
   636→				"content": change.Content,
   637→			},
   638→		})
   639→		messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{

   641→			"output": "File written successfully",
   642→		})
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{

   647→		"content": "Done!",
   648→	})

   653→	}

   667→	}

    16→// TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript tests that when Claude commits

    25→func TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript(t *testing.T) {

    27→

    17→// HookRunner executes CLI hooks in the test environment.
    18→type HookRunner struct {
    19→	RepoDir          string
    20→	ClaudeProjectDir string
    21→	T                interface {
    22→		Helper()
    23→		Fatalf(format string, args ...interface{})
    24→		Logf(format string, args ...interface{})
    25→	}
    26→}
    27→
    28→// NewHookRunner creates a new hook runner for the given repo directory.
    29→func NewHookRunner(repoDir, claudeProjectDir string, t interface {
    30→	Helper()
    31→	Fatalf(format string, args ...interface{})
    32→	Logf(format string, args ...interface{})
    33→}) *HookRunner {
    34→	return &HookRunner{
    35→		RepoDir:          repoDir,
    36→		ClaudeProjectDir: claudeProjectDir,
    37→		T:                t,
    38→	}
    39→}
    40→
    41→// HookResponse represents the JSON response from Claude Code hooks.
    42→type HookResponse struct {
    43→	Continue   bool   `json:"continue"`
    44→	StopReason string `json:"stopReason,omitempty"`
    45→}
    46→
    47→// SimulateUserPromptSubmit simulates the UserPromptSubmit hook.
    48→// This captures pre-prompt state (untracked files).
    49→func (r *HookRunner) SimulateUserPromptSubmit(sessionID string) error {
    50→	r.T.Helper()
    51→
    52→	input := map[string]string{
    53→		"session_id":      sessionID,
    54→		"transcript_path": "", // Not used for user-prompt-submit
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	return r.runHookWithInput("user-prompt-submit", input)
    58→}
    59→
    60→// SimulateUserPromptSubmitWithResponse simulates the UserPromptSubmit hook
    61→// and returns the parsed hook response (for testing blocking behavior).
    62→func (r *HookRunner) SimulateUserPromptSubmitWithResponse(sessionID string) (*HookResponse, error) {
    63→	r.T.Helper()
    64→
    65→	input := map[string]string{
    66→		"session_id":      sessionID,
    67→		"transcript_path": "", // Not used for user-prompt-submit
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	inputJSON, err := json.Marshal(input)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hook input: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	output := r.runHookWithOutput("user-prompt-submit", inputJSON)
    76→
    77→	// If hook failed with an error, return the error
    78→	if output.Err != nil {
    79→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("hook failed: %w\nStderr: %s\nStdout: %s",
    80→			output.Err, output.Stderr, output.Stdout)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Parse JSON response from stdout
    84→	var resp HookResponse
    85→	if len(output.Stdout) > 0 {
    86→		if err := json.Unmarshal(output.Stdout, &resp); err != nil {
    87→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook response: %w\nStdout: %s",
    88→				err, output.Stdout)
    89→		}
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	return &resp, nil
    93→}

   169→func (r *HookRunner) runHookWithInput(flag string, input interface{}) error {
   170→	r.T.Helper()
   171→
   172→	inputJSON, err := json.Marshal(input)
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hook input: %w", err)
